````text
# DS675 Mini-Project: reproducible BattLeDIM / L-Town evidence

Public supporting material for **Learning Models for Pressure Forecasting**, NJIT DS675.
The proposed task is recorded pressure at **n1, 15 minutes ahead**. This release addresses
the provenance questions raised after Milestone 2. **It reproduces descriptive analyses;
it does not train or evaluate the proposed learning models.**

## Start here

| Need | Open |
|---|---|
| Which claim or figure is supported by which code? | [Claim-to-evidence ledger](docs/CLAIM_EVIDENCE.md) |
| Who produced what, and with what assistance? | [Attribution and analysis status](docs/ATTRIBUTION.md) |
| Inspect the calculations step by step | [Executed explanatory notebook](notebooks/01_milestone2_evidence.ipynb) |
| See the recomputed numerical results | [EDA statistics](results/reproduced/eda_statistics.json), [verification](results/reproduced/verification.json) |
| Inspect Figure 4's complete underlying inventory | [47 source records](literature/inventory_sources.csv), [35 group assignments](literature/group_assignments.csv) |
| Read the papers and their redistribution status | [Literature index](literature/README.md) |
| Understand and run the material step by step | [Reproduction guide](docs/REPRODUCTION_GUIDE.md) |

## Reproduce from a fresh clone

Python **3.14** is the tested local environment. The pinned scientific dependencies are in
`requirements.txt`. Raw data are downloaded from the fixed original Zenodo record;
no institutional login is needed for those data.

```bash
git clone https://github.com/Andres-ArangoMartinez/DS675_MiniProject.git
cd DS675_MiniProject
python -m venv .venv
```

Select the environment's Python. On Windows PowerShell:

```powershell
.\.venv\Scripts\python.exe -m pip install -r requirements.txt
.\.venv\Scripts\python.exe reproduce.py --download
.\.venv\Scripts\python.exe -m unittest discover -s tests -v
```

On Linux/macOS, use `.venv/bin/python` in the same commands. Do not run the historical
source snapshots in `provenance/historical_scripts/`; they document the earlier work,
whereas `reproduce.py` is the portable entry point.

The first run downloads approximately 170 MB. Each of the 11 required files is verified
against its official MD5, then recorded with SHA-256. The CSVs are not duplicated in Git:
[data/source_manifest.json](data/source_manifest.json) gives the exact source URLs,
checksums, attribution and **CC-BY-4.0** license. A local flat cache can instead be supplied
with `--import-data /path/to/cache`. An altered file fails validation.

## Expected results

| Quantity | Recomputed value |
|---|---:|
| EDA period | January-September 2018 |
| Timestamps / complete days | 78,624 / 273 |
| Valid n1(+15) pairs | 78,621 |
| Persistence MAE | 0.05177624298851451 m |
| Persistence RMSE | 0.06849175638676988 m |
| n1 pressure range | 25.67-29.36 m |
| Nominal T1-to-n1 / n4 / n31 pipe distances | 891.3208 / 573.7565 / 307.7926 m |
| Reported rounded distances | 891 / 574 / 308 m |
| Historical literature inventory | 47 source records, 35 related-work groups |

These are **2018 descriptive results**, not final 2019 forecast scores. The pipeline reads
2019 only to check table structure, timestamps and missing values. It never fits a learned
model or computes a 2019 prediction score.

Outputs include the eight-table audit, correlations, sample forecast rows, full derived
tables (generated locally, ignored by Git), nominal pipe paths, regrouped literature counts,
Figures 2-4, package versions and input/code/output hashes. See
[results/reproduced/](results/reproduced/). The portable plots use DejaVu Serif; archived
report images retain their original bytes. Numerical reproduction does not imply pixel identity.

## Scope and limits

- This package was assembled and executed **with OpenAI Codex assistance**. It does not
  establish independent student implementation, understanding or review. See the attribution record.
- The 35-group inventory is a **historical, AI-assisted curated collection**, not an exhaustive
  systematic review. Recounting it does not repeat a historical search engine or validate every judgment.
- Ahmed et al. (2025) is a newly identified related study, **outside the historical 35-group count**.
  Its five signals and reconstruction objective must be considered in the next literature revision.
- Pipe distance is not pressure-propagation delay. A tank boundary does not prove upstream
  statistical independence. A fixed nominal PRV setting does not fix every remote pressure.
````

````text
# DS675 Mini-Project: reproducible BattLeDIM / L-Town evidence

Public supporting material for **Learning Models for Pressure Forecasting**, NJIT DS675.
The proposed task is recorded pressure at **n1, 15 minutes ahead**. This release addresses
the provenance questions raised after Milestone 2. **It reproduces descriptive analyses;
it does not train or evaluate the proposed learning models.**

## Start here

| Need | Open |
|---|---|
| Which claim or figure is supported by which code? | [Claim-to-evidence ledger](docs/CLAIM_EVIDENCE.md) |
| Who produced what, and with what assistance? | [Attribution and analysis status](docs/ATTRIBUTION.md) |
| Inspect the calculations step by step | [Executed explanatory notebook](notebooks/01_milestone2_evidence.ipynb) |
| See the recomputed numerical results | [EDA statistics](results/reproduced/eda_statistics.json), [verification](results/reproduced/verification.json) |
| Inspect Figure 4's complete underlying inventory | [47 source records](literature/inventory_sources.csv), [35 group assignments](literature/group_assignments.csv) |
| Read the papers and their redistribution status | [Literature index](literature/README.md) |
| Understand and run the material step by step | [Reproduction guide](docs/REPRODUCTION_GUIDE.md) |

## Professor feedback / where to look

The **2026-10-28 post-feedback engineering audit** adds source-checked discussion and a
future experimental plan. Interpretive prose is an AI-assisted draft pending student review;
automatic reproduction does not establish personal execution or understanding.

| Review need | Open |
|---|---|
| Corrections to the supplied engineering-rationale prompt | [Prompt audit](docs/PROMPT_VERIFICATION_AUDIT.md), [audited instructions](provenance/engineering_prompt/v2_1_verified_instructions.json) |
| Each professor criticism and its evidence | [Feedback response matrix](docs/FEEDBACK_RESPONSE_MATRIX.md) |
| What the small errors and correlations do / do not show | [Critical EDA discussion](docs/CRITICAL_EDA_DISCUSSION.md) |
| Area choice, n1 elevation and nominal/generator differences | [Sector and node rationale](docs/SECTOR_AND_NODE_SELECTION_RATIONALE.md) |
| Ahmed's actual task, preparation and published results | [Ahmed 2025 comparison](docs/AHMED_2025_COMPARISON.md) |
| Defensible contribution and falsifiable hypotheses | [Contribution](docs/RESEARCH_CONTRIBUTION.md), [engineering hypotheses](docs/ENGINEERING_TO_ML_HYPOTHESES.md) |
| Same-target feature comparisons, chronology and scope | [Proposed E0-E4 plan](docs/EXPERIMENTAL_CONTRIBUTION_PLAN.md) |
| Prior field paper and verified daily-overlay convention | [Prior research / method transfer](docs/PRIOR_RESEARCH_AND_METHOD_TRANSFER.md) |
| Actual human review still needed | [Student review worksheet](docs/STUDENT_REVIEW_LOG.md) |
| AI discovery versus student-reported screening and closer reading | [Review method and student role](literature/REVIEW_METHOD_AND_STUDENT_ROLE.md) |
| Six original studies, source-checked facts and comparison limits | [Six-study critical matrix](literature/SIX_STUDY_CRITICAL_MATRIX.md), [V3 provenance audit](docs/LITERATURE_PROVENANCE_V3_AUDIT.md) |
| Reviewable response and recruitment text, not sent/posted | [Professor response](docs/PROFESSOR_RESPONSE_DRAFT.md), [Milestone 3 group pitch](docs/MILESTONE3_GROUP_PITCH.md) |
| Commands and verification actually performed | [Engineering validation record](provenance/engineering_validation.json) |

The optional [static generator audit](scripts/audit_engineering_rationale.py) checks official
geometry without training models or evaluating 2019. It confirms the selected elevations,
but pipe distances and tank diameter differ from the nominal model. Existing report
distances remain explicitly nominal. The historical PDF and 35-group inventory are preserved.

The **V3 literature-provenance update** records the student's reported abstract/section
screening and closer examination of six studies separately from AI-assisted discovery.
The new source checks do not certify historical personal reading or reproduce author models.
The supplied first-person replacement paragraph remains unpublished pending confirmation;
public documentation uses an attributed third-person account. See the
[V3 validation record](provenance/literature_v3_validation.json) for checks actually performed.

## Reproduce from a fresh clone

Python **3.14** is the tested local environment. The pinned scientific dependencies are in
`requirements.txt`. Raw data are downloaded from the fixed original Zenodo record;
no institutional login is needed for those data.

```bash
git clone https://github.com/Andres-ArangoMartinez/DS675_MiniProject.git
cd DS675_MiniProject
python -m venv .venv
```
````
